Run from the repository root with the lab prerequisites from README. Use a new output directory. Do not automatically retry an uncertain launch.

python3 -m resmgrcheck replay docs/evidence/phase7/executions/sensor-fixed/replay.json --target lab --out runs/replay-new

Original selected deployment: 38ebbf253df0bacce8a52cf7572fcb1ff83b19a54621023a3543f789ccda9084. Native source/build and hashes are shown in the summary.

Without candidate selection, workflow replay retains the original immutable deployment. To test an actual source correction, build and deploy it, then add --candidate-manifest build/qnx-x86_64/manifest.json. Keep the saved contract and fixture setting unchanged. Changing fixture mode is a disclosed incomparable contrast.

Evidence and instrumentation

Managed examples use explicit lab pending/unblock/state and ordering instrumentation. Deliberate defect settings remain disclosed and are checked against the corrected contract. These directed tests do not establish exhaustive concurrency or absence of memory leaks.
